Second, based on the observation that important neurons re-fire periodically during long reasoning generation, we introduce an activation memory that accumulates importance across update phases so that recurring neurons are retained.
  Experiments on diverse reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that our method outperforms the previous state-of-the-art adaptive pruning method by 39.7 percentage points in average accuracy at batch size 4 with 50% target sparsity on DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-7B, and reaches 1.40x speedup over dense inference at 50% actual sparsity.

</details>


### [88] [S2Dialog: Multimodal Dialogue Retrieval with Semantic and Acoustic-Style Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14029)
*Xueqi Wang,Zhigang Wang,Runqing Zhang,Zhenqi Jia,Junfeng Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出S2Dialog，一个用于多模态对话库中对话级语义-风格检索的统一框架，通过对话级文本检索器和对话级声学检索器分别编码对话的文本与声学模态，并引入对话级文本-声学对比学习来对齐语义和风格相似的对话，在DailyTalk数据集上取得了优异的检索性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检索方法局限于话语级（utterance-level）或单模态（unimodal）匹配，难以捕捉整个对话的全局语义连贯性和风格一致性，无法满足对话级检索任务的需求。

Method: 提出S2Dialog框架，包含两个核心组件：1）对话级文本检索器（Dialogue-level Textual Retriever）和对话级声学检索器（Dialogue-level Acoustic Retriever），分别将对话的文本和声学模态编码为对话级表示；2）对话级文本-声学对比学习（Dialogue-level Textual-Acoustic Contrastive Learning），用于对齐语义和风格相似的对话并区分不相关的对话。

Result: 在多模态对话数据集DailyTalk上的大量实验表明，S2Dialog取得了优异的检索性能。

Conclusion: S2Dialog有效填补了对话级多模态检索的空白，能够同时捕捉对话的语义连贯性和风格一致性，为对话相关下游任务（如对话中的情感识别、口语对话系统、对话语音合成）提供有价值的参考。

Abstract: Multimodal dialogue retrieval aims to retrieve dialogues from multimodal dialogue banks that are similar to a target dialogue in terms of both textual semantics and acoustic conversational styles. Such dialogue-level retrieval is crucial for many dialogue-related tasks, including Emotion Recognition in Conversation, Spoken Dialogue Systems, and Conversational Speech Synthesis, where external dialogue examples can provide valuable semantic and stylistic references. However, existing retrieval methods are still largely limited to utterance-level or unimodal matching, and often fail to capture the global semantic coherence and stylistic consistency of an entire dialogue. To address this gap, we propose S2Dialog, a unified framework for dialogue-level semantic-style retrieval from multimodal dialogue banks. Specifically, S2Dialog consists of a Dialogue-level Textual Retriever and a Dialogue-level Acoustic Retriever, which encode the textual and acoustic modalities of a dialogue into dialogue-level representations, respectively. To further enhance multimodal retrieval, we introduce Dialogue-level Textual-Acoustic Contrastive Learning, which aligns semantically and stylistically similar dialogues while distinguishing unrelated ones. Extensive experiments on the multimodal dialogue dataset DailyTalk demonstrate that S2Dialog achieves outstanding retrieval performance.

</details>


### [89] [HERMES: a multi-agent framework for structured knowledge extraction from ultra-long documents in geoscience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14055)
*Ziqi Song,Zongyuan Xiang,James G. Ogg,Bruce S. Lieberman,Gabi Ogg,Natalia López Carranza,Wen Du,Yufei Ye,Shuan Li,Zhong Peng,Shaoqi Yu,Juye Wei,Ying Zhou,Jieping Ye,Jiang Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出HERMES，一个可扩展的多智能体框架，用于从超长科学文档中提取结构化数据。系统应用于55卷《无脊椎古生物学专论》，成功构建了包含32,277个化石分类实体和451,878个属性的结构化数据库，并以平均F1分数约0.90（实体）和0.91（属性）的稳定性能运行，比完全人工基线效率提高约六倍，且无需额外训练即可迁移至古地磁学和地球化学领域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地球科学的权威科学知识大多被困在传统专著和历史文献中，非结构化的文本和复杂版式阻碍了计算访问。现有方法难以处理超长科学文档的结构化数据提取，缺乏可扩展且可移植的解决方案。

Method: HERMES采用协调大型语言模型（LLM）的多智能体框架，在统一的文档级提取过程中整合领域约束、验证规则和证据追踪，同时融合解析后的文本、表格、图形和标题等多种信息来源，实现超长科学文档的结构化数据提取。

Result: 在55卷《无脊椎古生物学专论》上构建了包含32,277个化石分类实体和451,878个属性的数据库，平均F1分数实体约0.90、属性约0.91，跨化石类群性能稳定，每卷效率比人工基线提高约六倍；在古地磁学和地球化学上的零训练评估证明了跨领域可迁移性。

Conclusion: 该工作为将历史科学文献转化为FAIR导向的结构化数据提供了可行路径，为数据密集型学科和大规模知识整合建立了可持续的基础设施。

Abstract: Authoritative scientific knowledge in geoscience remains largely trapped in legacy monographs and historical literature, where unstructured text and complex layouts hinder computational access. We introduce HERMES, a scalable multi-agent framework that extracts structured data from ultra-long scientific documents. Using a coordinating large language model, HERMES integrates domain constraints, validation rules and evidence tracing within a unified document-level extraction process that incorporates parsed text, tables, figures and captions. Applied to the 55-volume Treatise on Invertebrate Paleontology, the system produced a structured database of 32,277 fossil taxonomic entities and 451,878 attributes, released online at https://treatise.geolex.org. Extraction performance remained stable across fossil groups (average F1 scores of approximately 0.90 for entities and 0.91 for attributes), improving per-volume efficiency approximately sixfold relative to the tested fully manual baseline. Evaluation in palaeomagnetism and geochemistry, conducted without additional model training, demonstrated transfer across distinct geoscience domains. This work provides a practical pathway to transform historical scientific literature into FAIR-oriented structured data, offering a sustainable infrastructure for data-intensive disciplines and large-scale knowledge integration.

</details>


### [90] [The conditional superiority of fast silicon sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14079)
*Nickolas Hock Yuen Lam,Ji Xuan Voo,Xiangyu Ma*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究评估了"快速"与"慢速"硅采样模式在新加坡全国代表性调查样本中的算法保真度，发现硅采样仍处于早期发展阶段需谨慎使用，但快速模式在计算效率和算法保真度上均优于传统慢速模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硅采样偶尔能产生令人惊讶的良好人口估计，但尚不清楚加速这一过程是否会削弱其保真度。作者希望系统评估快速与慢速硅采样模式的算法保真度差异。

Method: 采用新加坡全国代表性调查受访者样本，比较"快速"和"慢速"两种硅采样模式的算法保真度，包括对总体均值的估计能力、观点方差估计以及对潜在语境空间的还原度。

Result: 硅样本能产生中等保真度的总体均值估计，但持续低估观点方差并扭曲人类观点背后的潜在语境空间。快速硅采样在计算资源和运行时间上显著更高效，且在算法保真度上单调优于慢速采样。

Conclusion: 当代前沿模型的硅采样仍是早期开发中的方法，应谨慎使用。然而在现有局限性下，快速硅采样模式相对优于传统慢速模式，是更值得采用的策略。

Abstract: Silicon sampling can produce surprisingly good population estimates at times. Does doing it fast attenuate such fidelity? In this study, we extend and assess ongoing work in silicon sampling by comparing the algorithmic fidelity of "fast" and "slow" modes of silicon sampling among a nationally representative sample of Singaporean survey respondents. We find that silicon sampling with contemporary frontier models remains a method in early development to be used only with great caution. While silicon samples are able to produce moderately faithful estimates of population means, they continue to understate opinion variance and distort the latent contextual space behind human opinions. Conditional on such limitations, we find "fast" modes of silicon sampling to be relatively superior to traditional "slow" modes of silicon sampling. Fast silicon sampling is significantly more efficient in compute resources and run-time while being monotonically superior to slower modes of sampling in algorithmic fidelity.

</details>


### [91] [Leading-Silence Augmentation and Multi-Stage Synthetic Supervision for the Second MLC-SLM Challenge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14150)
*Kexin Shi,Renhe Sun,Yuge Huang,Ximeng Wang,Jiayi Zhou,Jian Liu,Malu Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文描述了第二届多语言会话语音语言模型（MLC-SLM）挑战赛中的两项任务解决方案：针对说话人日志与识别任务（Task 1），通过对VibeVoice-ASR-7B模型采用随机前置静音裁剪、时间戳一致性修正和指数移动平均（EMA）训练策略，将tcpMER从18.30%降至16.73%；针对会话语音理解任务（Task 2），通过多模态候选生成、静音音频过滤和分布匹配增强构造合成问答对，并微调Qwen3-Omni-30B-A3B-Instruct进行带标签的直接回答，将准确率从83.0%提升至86.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决多语言完整未分段会话中缺乏话语边界和说话人标签的挑战：Task 1需要在无先验边界条件下进行说话人日志与识别，Task 2在无问答训练集条件下进行会话语音理解。

Method: Task 1：对VibeVoice-ASR-7B采用随机前置静音裁剪、一致时间戳修正和EMA训练策略进行微调。Task 2：通过多模态候选生成构造合成问答对，结合静音音频过滤和分布匹配增强技术，微调Qwen3-Omni-30B-A3B-Instruct进行带标签的直接回答。

Result: Task 1：裁剪使tcpMER从18.30%降至17.27%，EMA进一步降至16.73%。Task 2：联合应用分布匹配增强和带标签直接回答使准确率从83.0%提升至86.0%。

Conclusion: 所提出的数据增强和训练策略在两项任务上均取得有效提升，证明了随机裁剪与EMA策略对会话日志识别的改进作用，以及合成问答对生成与分布匹配增强对会话理解准确率的提升效果。

Abstract: The second Multilingual Conversational Speech Language Model (MLC-SLM) Challenge evaluates two tasks over complete, unsegmented multilingual conversations: speaker diarization and recognition (Task 1) and conversational speech understanding (Task 2). Neither task provides oracle utterance boundaries or speaker labels at evaluation, and Task 2 provides no question-answer training set. For Task 1, we fine-tune VibeVoice-ASR-7B with random leading-silence cropping, consistent timestamp correction, and an exponential moving average (EMA) training strategy. For Task 2, we construct synthetic question-answer pairs through multimodal candidate generation, silent-audio filtering, and distribution-matched augmentation, and fine-tune Qwen3-Omni-30B-A3B-Instruct for tagged direct answering. On the Task 1 evaluation set, cropping reduces tcpMER from 18.30% to 17.27%, and EMA further reduces it to 16.73%. On the Task 2 evaluation set, jointly applying distribution-matched augmentation and tagged direct answering raises accuracy from 83.0% to 86.0%.

</details>


### [92] [How Much Do Legal RAG Systems Still Hallucinate?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14210)
*Souvick Das,Sallam Abualhaija,Domenico Bianculli*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文对法律领域八个RAG系统在两个法律语料库（英文GDPR和法国民法典）上的幻觉行为进行了细粒度分析，采用声明级和答案级评估，发现幻觉仍然普遍存在，最差系统中近一半的响应包含幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 法律领域中RAG系统的幻觉问题可能导致严重后果，但目前对此缺乏精细的理解和系统性的分析，需要深入了解幻觉在不同系统、语料库和问题类型中的表现。

Method: 对八个法律RAG系统在两个法律语料库（英文GDPR和法国民法典）上进行幻觉行为的细粒度分析，采用声明级（claim-level）和答案级（answer-level）评估，报告幻觉密度和严重程度，分析不同问题类别和用户角色下的表现，并在142个法律专家撰写的问题上验证发现。

Result: 幻觉仍然普遍存在，最佳系统的幻觉率低于10%，而最差系统几乎一半的响应存在幻觉。此外，包含错误假设的假前提（false-premise）问题在人工撰写的问题集中产生了较高的幻觉率。

Conclusion: 法律RAG系统的幻觉问题依然严峻，特别是面对包含错误前提的问题时，系统的可靠性不足，需要进一步改进。

Abstract: Hallucination is a major challenge for retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) systems in the legal domain, where ungrounded answers can lead to serious consequences. To better understand this problem, we conduct a fine-grained analysis of hallucination behavior in eight legal RAG systems across two legal corpora, the GDPR (in English) and a national civil law (in French). Using claim-level and answer-level evaluation, we report on hallucination density and severity, analyze performance across question categories and user personas, and validate our findings on an independent set of 142 legal-expert-authored questions. Our results show that hallucinations remain pervasive, ranging from less than 10% of responses for the best-performing systems to nearly half in the worst case. We further find that false-premise questions, containing incorrect assumptions that must be rejected, produce high hallucination rates on the manually-drafted questions.

</details>


### [93] [The More Popular, The Harder to Forget: Adaptive Popularity for LLM Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14229)
*Anna Borisiuk,Andrey Savchenko,Alexander Panchenko,Elena Tutubalina*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出AdaPop（自适应流行度）方法，针对LLM遗忘任务中"高频事实比低频事实更顽固"的问题，利用局部token置信度与基于外部代理（如Wikidata链接数、LLM作为裁判）的流行度相关指数，结合双上升控制器动态调节遗忘-保留平衡。实验证明其遗忘效果显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有个LLM遗忘方法对所有训练数据施加统一的梯度压力，忽略了训练数据频率的影响——事实上高频流行事实在预训练中记忆更深、更难被遗忘。这种"一刀切"的策略导致遗忘效果不佳，需要针对事实流行度自适应的遗忘策略。

Method: AdaPop方法包含三个核心组件：(1) 局部token置信度用于衡量模型对每个事实的记忆强度；(2) 每个事实的流行度相关指数，由外部代理（如Wikidata sitelinks或LLM-as-Judge）计算得到；(3) 双上升控制器，通过每轮调整保留惩罚项来自动平衡"遗忘"与"保留"两个目标。该方法将流行的（高频）事实施加更强遗忘压力，而稀有事实则相对温和处理。

Result: 在三个模型家族和两个基准上，AdaPop在释义查询下比竞争方法少泄露约5倍的被遗忘内容，在对抗性改写下少泄露约1.6倍。内部指标显示，使用AdaPop时遗忘集的隐藏状态比预遗忘模型的状态偏离更远，而保留集的表示保持接近。

Conclusion: 基于事实流行度的自适应遗忘策略能显著提升LLM遗忘的有效性，同时保持对保留数据的完整性。通过外部代理估计流行度并动态平衡遗忘-保留目标，AdaPop为LLM遗忘提供了一种更精细、更有效的方案。

Abstract: Popular facts are memorised more deeply during pretraining and resist removal longer than rare ones, yet existing LLM unlearning methods apply uniform gradient pressure regardless of training-data frequency. We propose the AdaPop (Adaptive Popularity) method, which combines local token confidence with a per-fact popularity-dependent exponent derived from an external proxy (e.g., Wikidata sitelinks, LLM-as-Judge), and automates the forget-retain balance via a dual-ascent controller that adjusts the retain penalty each epoch. Across three model families and two benchmarks, AdaPop leaks ~5x less forgotten content than competing methods under paraphrased queries and ~1.6x less under adversarial reformulations. We support our analysis with internal metrics: under our method, forget-set hidden states move further from the pre-unlearning model's states than under other methods, while retain-set representations remain close.

</details>


### [94] [SimpleOPD: Simple Tokenizer-Agnostic On-Policy Distillation for Long-Context Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14277)
*Haonan He,Haodi Lei,Yun Luo,Haoran Zhang,Shunkai Zhang,Yizhuo Li,Shengji Tang,Zhilin Wang,Runzhe Zhan,Lei Bai,Ganqu Cui,Fangchen Yu,Yafu Li,Peng Ye,Ning Ding,Yu Cheng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了将长上下文推理教师模型（SU-01）的推理能力通过同策略蒸馏（OPD）迁移到短上下文学生模型的方法，通过在共享文本空间对齐token、引入学生参考KL损失并掩蔽终止token优势，有效解决了分词器不匹配、分布偏移和长度爆炸等挑战，在多个模型族上取得了显著的数学推理提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同策略蒸馏（OPD）是迁移大模型推理能力的有效途径，但将其从长上下文推理教师模型迁移到短上下文学生模型时面临实际操作挑战，包括分词器不匹配、师生分布偏移、响应长度爆炸和训练不稳定等问题，需要系统性地理解和解决这些挑战。

Method: 1) 在共享文本空间执行OPD，仅对齐学生和教师分词器下占据相同文本跨度的token，以解决分词器差异；2) 引入学生参考KL损失，并在特殊终止token（如</think>和<|im_end|>）上掩蔽优势值，约束学生不过度偏离初始策略，缓解师生分布偏移并促进长度稳定增长。

Result: 在Qwen3、Qwen3.5、Intern-S2、GLM-4.7、Gemma-4等不同模型族上均获得一致的数学推理提升，尤其在自然语言数学证明任务中表现突出。Intern-S2-Preview在ProofBench上提升21.2分（达到55.2，超越Gemini-2.5-Pro），在HLE和HiPhO等科学基准上也有提升，表明OPD迁移的推理能力可泛化到数学训练领域之外。

Conclusion: 通过OPD将长上下文推理能力迁移到短上下文学生模型是可行且有效的，所提出的共享文本空间对齐策略和长度控制机制成功解决了实践中的关键挑战，迁移的推理能力具有跨领域的泛化性。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) offers a promising way to transfer reasoning capabilities from stronger teacher models, but applying it to long-context reasoning teachers and short-context students introduces practical challenges, including tokenizer mismatch, teacher-student distribution mismatch, response length explosion, and training instability. In this work, we study this setting by transferring proof-reasoning capabilities from the long-context reasoning model SU-01 to short-context student models. To handle tokenizer differences, we perform OPD in a shared text space and align only tokens that occupy identical text spans under the student and teacher tokenizers. To mitigate the problem of excessive generation length and frequent truncation, we introduce a student reference KL loss and mask the advantages of special termination tokens such as </think> and <|im_end|>. This strategy constrains the student from drifting excessively from its initial policy, thereby mitigating the teacher-student distribution mismatch problem and fostering steady length growth. Experiments on both same-family and different-family student models, including Qwen3, Qwen3.5, Intern-S2, GLM-4.7, Gemma-4, show consistent gains in mathematical reasoning, especially natural-language math proving. Notably, Intern-S2-Preview improves by 21.2 points on ProofBench, reaching 55.2 and surpassing Gemini-2.5-Pro. It also improves on science benchmarks such as HLE and HiPhO, suggesting that OPD transfers reasoning capabilities that generalize beyond the mathematical training domain.

</details>


### [95] [Envs-FORGE: Frontier-Optimized Reward-Grounded Environment Synthesis for Agent RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14312)
*Xiaojun Wu,Cehao Yang,Honghao Liu,Xueyuan Lin,Zhichao Shi,Hao Zhou,Xuhui Jiang,Chengjin Xu,Jia Li,Jian Guo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Envs-FORGE是一种将验证器奖励转换为逐种子环境合成动作的提示策略，通过MILP优化选择环境合成方向，在多个基准上显著提升了RL终端智能体的Pass@1性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的固定配方（如few-shot、Self-Instruct、Evol-Instruct）对所有种子应用相同的提示策略，忽略了不同种子在训练过程中可能需要不同难度的任务。RL终端智能体需要可执行、奖励可靠且难度适中的训练环境。

Method: Envs-FORGE将验证器奖励转化为逐种子环境合成动作：估计种子通过率，围绕目标学习前沿评估六种投影方向动作，并通过逐种子混合整数线性规划（MILP）选择最优动作。所选动作驱动指令、fixtures、oracle解决方案、测试和Docker环境的同步重写，仅验证通过的bundle进入RL训练。MILP形式还支持可选的软技能覆盖用于组合规划。

Result: 在Qwen 3.5 35B上，tb-core的Pass@1从40.0%提升至49.2%（+9.2个百分点），tb-2.0从23.0%提升至29.4%（+6.4个百分点），分别超过最强固定配方基线2.4和2.1个百分点。SWE-bench Verified达到77.1%（Base为73.4%），在4B-35B跨模型规模上tb-core提升6.8-9.2个百分点。

Conclusion: Envs-FORGE通过动态、个性化的环境合成策略优于静态固定配方，同时保持相同的训练集规模和操作成本，证明了自适应环境合成在RL终端智能体训练中的有效性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) for terminal agents needs executable training environments with reliable rewards and useful difficulty. Fixed recipes such as few-shot, Self-Instruct, and Evol-Instruct apply the same prompting policy to every seed, even when the current policy would benefit from a harder, easier, or simply different task. We present Envs-FORGE, a prompting policy that converts verifier rewards into per-seed environment-synthesis actions. Envs-FORGE estimates seed pass rates, scores six projection--direction actions around a target learning frontier, and solves a per-seed mixed-integer linear program (MILP) to choose the action that conditions generation. The selected action drives synchronized rewriting of the instruction, fixtures, oracle solution, tests, and Docker environment; only gold-verified bundles enter RL training. The indexed MILP form also supports optional soft skill coverage for portfolio planning. On Qwen 3.5 35B, Envs-FORGE improves Pass@1 over Base by 9.2 percentage points on tb-core (40.0% to 49.2%) and 6.4 points on tb-2.0 (23.0% to 29.4%), exceeding the strongest fixed-recipe baseline by 2.4 and 2.1 points. It reaches 77.1% on SWE-bench Verified versus 73.4% for Base, and improves tb-core by 6.8--9.2 points across the evaluated 4B--35B models. All synthesis methods export 100 verified environments and use 2.27M--2.88M synthesis tokens, placing the comparison at the same downstream training-set size and the same operational scale. The source code is available at https://github.com/DataArcTech/DataArc-SynData-Toolkit/.

</details>


### [96] [Local and Global Regimes of Geometric Complexity in Language Model Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14361)
*Arwa Osman,Marco Baroni,Iuri Macocco*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了数据集词汇多样性（唯一末词数量）对语言模型表示内在维度（ID）估计的影响，发现存在尺度依赖的双重机制转换，并推导出精确的无参数公式预测转换点。研究警示ID不能直接作为表示复杂度的指标，同时揭示了LLM内部流形结构的一般组织原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 内在维度（ID）被广泛用于探测语言模型的表示复杂度，但ID差异究竟反映了语言本身的属性还是数据集构建过程的人为产物，这一点尚不明确。作者希望厘清词汇多样性这一数据构造因素对ID估计的影响，以正确解读ID的含义。

Method: 聚焦于数据集词汇多样性（即数据集中唯一末词项的数目）对ID估计的影响。通过对不同词汇多样性水平的条件进行系统性实验，观察两种ID机制之间的尺度依赖转换，并推导出一个精确的、无参数的公式来确定发生这种反转的临界点，且该公式在所有测试尺度下均与观测到的转换点吻合。

Result: 发现两种机制间的尺度依赖转换：在低词汇多样性条件下，唯一末词更少的情形产生更高的ID；而在高词汇多样性时，顺序反转，唯一词更多的情形产生更高的ID。推导出的无参数公式在所有测试尺度下均精确匹配观测到的转换点。

Conclusion: 一方面，将表示集合的内在维度直接解读为复杂度线索时需要格外谨慎；另一方面，发现的双重ID机制揭示了LLM中语言数据组织的一般性原则，为理解其内部流形结构提供了新视角。

Abstract: Intrinsic dimensionality (ID) is widely used to probe the representational complexity of language models, but it remains unclear whether ID differences reflect properties of language itself or artefacts of how the underlying dataset was constructed. In this paper, we focus specifically on how lexical diversity, the number of unique last-token items present in a dataset, affects ID estimates of that dataset. We find a scale-dependent transition between two regimes: at low lexical diversity, conditions with fewer unique final words produce higher ID, while at high lexical diversity, this ordering reverses, and conditions with more unique words produce higher ID. We derive an exact, parameter-free formula for the point at which this reversal occurs, which matches the observed transition point at every scale tested. On the one hand, our results highlight how care must be taken when interpreting the intrinsic dimensionality of a set of representations as a straightforward cue of their complexity. On the other hand, our discovery of the two ID regimes reveals a general principle of organisation of linguistic data in LLMs that sheds new light on their inner manifold structures.

</details>


### [97] [A Survey of Large Models in Sports](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14377)
*Yichen Xu,Jianzhe Ma,Chuhan Wang,Zhonghao Cao,Liangyu Chen,Wenxuan Wang,Qin Jin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文全面调研了大模型（特别是多模态大语言模型 MLLM）在体育领域的研究现状，涵盖不同参与者群体下的任务与应用概览、体育相关数据集与基准的详细分析，以及当前挑战与未来方向的批判性讨论，旨在为大模型驱动的体育智能研究与实践奠定基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 体育在全球范围内日益受到关注，是促进身体健康、文化交流、社会联系和经济增长的重要力量。大模型（尤其是多模态大语言模型）的发展为体育的理解、分析和交互带来了变革性潜力，但目前缺乏系统性的综述来梳理这一快速发展的交叉领域。

Method: 本文采用综合性调研方法，从三个层面展开：(i) 按不同参与者群体（如运动员、教练、观众、裁判等）梳理大模型在体育中的任务与应用；(ii) 对体育相关的数据集和基准进行全面分析；(iii) 批判性讨论当前面临的挑战，并展望未来研究方向。同时维护开源的GitHub资源仓库。

Result: 文章系统性地整理了大模型在体育领域的研究进展，覆盖了跨多个参与者群体的应用场景，分析了相关数据集与基准的现状，并指出了该领域当前存在的关键挑战与未来发展方向。

Conclusion: 本文旨在为大模型驱动的体育智能研究与实践建立基础性框架，推动该交叉领域进一步发展和落地应用。

Abstract: Sports have witnessed growing global enthusiasm in recent years, serving as a vital force for physical health, cultural exchange, social connection, and economic growth. The rapid advancement of large models, particularly (multimodal) large language models (M)LLMs, has demonstrated transformative potential to reshape sports understanding, analysis, and interaction across diverse domains. This paper presents a comprehensive survey of large models in sports, including (i) an overview of tasks and applications across different participant groups; (ii) a detailed analysis of sports-related datasets and benchmarks; and (iii) a critical discussion of current challenges and future directions. Our goal is to establish a foundation for advancing research and practical development of large-model-driven sports intelligence. An open-source GitHub repository is maintained at: https://github.com/Road2Redemption/Awesome_Large_Models_In_Sports1.

</details>


### [98] [Information Satisfaction: A Reader-Centered Axis for Summarization Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14457)
*Isabel Cachola,William Walden,Reno Kriz,Mark Dredze*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文指出现有摘要评估指标（如ROUGE、BERTScore）无法衡量摘要对个体用户的信息效用，因为不同用户（如研究者与医生）的信息需求不同，而查询聚焦摘要仅靠短查询难以区分这些需求。作者通过扰动测试和专家人工评估发现，包括LLM-as-judge在内的主流指标对信息内容差异不敏感，且与人类基于用户背景的信息满意度判断一致性很差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有摘要评估指标仅关注通用质量（如ROUGE、BERTScore）或特定属性（如可读性、事实性），但忽略了摘要对具体用户的实用性。查询聚焦摘要无法仅凭简短查询捕捉用户全部需求，而读者的身份/专业背景（persona）相对稳定，可补充这一缺失上下文。作者希望验证现有指标是否能反映这一用户维度上的信息满意度差异。

Method: 作者采用了两种评估方法：(1) 对信息内容进行扰动测试，检验流行指标（包括LLM-as-judge指标）对信息差异的敏感性；(2) 进行专家人工评估，基于特定用户的背景和使用场景衡量摘要的信息满足度偏好，并与传统指标和基于LLM的指标进行对比。

Result: 实验发现：(1) 许多流行指标（包括强LLM-as-judge指标）无法通过基础的信息内容扰动测试；(2) 传统指标和LLM-based指标都无法充分衡量信息满意度，与人类基于用户背景的判断的一致性很差。

Conclusion: 现有摘要评估指标在衡量摘要对具体用户的信息效用方面存在显著不足，需要开发考虑读者persona和背景信息的新评估方法。

Abstract: The majority of work on summarization evaluation focuses on general summary quality (e.g., ROUGE, BERTScore) or specific desired properties (e.g., readability, factuality). However, these metrics fail to measure the utility of a summary to an individual user. For example, a biomedical researcher learning about the latest vaccine research will have different informational needs from a family doctor. Query-focused summarization captures part of this need, but in practice, users rarely state everything relevant in a query: a single short query is likely inadequate to distinguish the needs of a researcher from those of a physician. By contrast, a reader's background or persona (their role and expertise) is comparatively stable across queries and recovers much of this missing context, which makes it a practical signal for assessing whether a summary satisfies that reader's needs. In this work, we assess how sensitive popular summarization metrics are to both informational and persona differences, and find that many popular metrics, including strong LLM-as-judge metrics, fail basic perturbation tests of informational content. We additionally conduct an expert human evaluation, measuring summary preferences based on information satisfaction given a specific person's background and use case. We find that both traditional and LLM-based metrics are insufficient measures of information satisfaction and agree poorly with human judgment.

</details>


### [99] [You Only Pass Once: Answering and Abstaining Together in a Single Forward Pass of a Frozen Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14465)
*Ziyang Luo,Zhongyao Chu,Xinjie He,Youting Wang,Xukui Qin,Runxiong Wu,Yan-Syuan Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出YOPO（You Only Pass Once）系统，在冻结的大语言模型（Qwen2.5 1.5B/3B/7B）单次前向传播中同时实现答案生成、可控推理（steering）与拒答（abstention）。核心思想是：在写方向的steering probe和读方向的sufficiency direction之间，训练一个小网络重构steering前残差流状态，使两者不互相干扰，从而避免双前向传播的开销。端到端三路准确率大幅超越冻结基线（0.375→0.798@1.5B alphaNLI），并在所有规模下超越两遍参考方法，且提供了首个answer-or-abstain基准套件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冻结语言模型在推理任务上有两个耦合缺陷：一是未能充分利用残差流中已编码的证据；二是当输入信息不足时无法察觉而胡编乱造。已有的两个研究方向（conditioning steering probe用于写残差流提升推理，zero-shot sufficiency direction用于读残差流检测信息不足）分别处理这两个问题，但在同一前向传播中部署它们会互相干扰，而分开两次前向传播开销翻倍。因此需要一种机制让读写两方向在单次前向传播中共存。

Method: 保持sufficiency direction固定，训练一个小网络以（steered, clean）残差对的均方误差为目标，学习从steering写入后的残差重构出steering前的原始残差；然后在重构结果上读取固定的sufficiency direction进行拒答判断。整个系统YOPO在冻结的Qwen2.5骨干上一次前向传播内同时完成回答、steering和abstain。

Result: 端到端三路准确率从冻结基线的0.375提升到0.798（1.5B alphaNLI）；单次前向传播在所有规模上超越两遍参考方法（0.798/0.830/0.893 vs 0.753/0.790/0.863），并在十个骨干（六个模型家族）上验证。绘制了capacity-transfer frontier，源侧审计发现alphaNLI构造泄漏表面伪影，因此架构结论锚定在原生标签复现（SQuAD2、RepLiQA、MuSiQue）上。在标准四域套件上贡献了首个answer-or-abstain基准，其gate在每个域内数据集上达到最优，无标签方向是唯一能跨域迁移的gate族。

Conclusion: YOPO证明通过重构策略可在单次前向传播中同时实现推理增强与拒答能力，打破了两遍前向传播的必要性。Abstention不应被直接训练进去（capacity-transfer frontier量化了这一原则）；同时强调了在构建拒答基准时需警惕表面伪影泄漏，应基于原生标注数据验证。

Abstract: A frozen language model on reasoning tasks has two coupled weaknesses: it under-uses evidence its own residual stream already encodes, and it fails to detect when the input is insufficient to answer, so it confabulates. This paper consolidates two research lines that address these on the same residual stream: a conditional steering probe writes the stream at mid-stack layers and recovers reasoning accuracy from a frozen backbone, and a zero-shot sufficiency direction reads the stream and abstains when information is insufficient. Deployed in one forward pass they interfere: the steering write shifts the state the direction reads, costing up to 8 AUROC points of cross-domain transfer on small models; a separate clean pass doubles inference cost. We keep the direction fixed and train a small network to reconstruct the pre-steering residual from the steered one -- mean-squared error on (steered, clean) pairs, no sufficiency labels -- and read the direction on the reconstruction. The resulting system, YOPO (You Only Pass Once), answers, steers, and abstains in one forward pass of a frozen Qwen2.5 backbone (1.5B/3B/7B). End to end, three-way accuracy more than doubles the frozen baseline (0.375->0.798 on 1.5B alphaNLI) and one pass beats the two-pass reference at every scale (0.798/0.830/0.893 vs 0.753/0.790/0.863) and on ten backbones across six model families. We chart the capacity-transfer frontier quantifying the principle that abstention should not be trained in; a source-side audit catches our own alphaNLI construction leaking a surface artifact, so architectural claims are anchored on native-label replications (SQuAD2, RepLiQA, MuSiQue); and on the standard four-domain suite we contribute, to our knowledge, the first answer-or-abstain benchmark, where our gate tops every in-domain dataset and the label-free direction is the only gate family to survive domain transfer.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [100] ["I Thought You Were The Uncensored Place": Norms, Rules, and Moderation in AI-Generated Sexual Content Communities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13659)
*Lucy Qin,Jaron Mink,Elissa M. Redmiles*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 通过访谈24名大型AI生成性内容（AIG-SC）在线社区成员与版主，研究社区治理如何在支持自由表达的同时防止滥用行为，发现反审查和非评判规范使版主难以有效执行规则，建议从技术、社区和法律三方面协同治理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI生成性内容的快速增长，相关在线社区不断涌现，但缺乏对社区治理机制如何平衡自由表达与防止滥用（如AI生成的CSAM）的系统性研究。

Method: 对24名来自10,000+成员大型AIG-SC社区的成员和版主进行深度访谈，分析社区形成原因、隐性规范、明确规则及其内容审核实施情况，以及社区价值观与审核之间的张力。

Result: 发现尽管许多创作者和社区对滥用行为设有个人底线，但创建任何形式的AI生成性内容的建议和资源对所有人开放，不受意图限制。反审查和非评判的社区规范使版主只能依赖法律和平台服务条款来证明其行为的正当性，造成规则执行困难。

Conclusion: 有效缓解AI生成性内容滥用的治理需要技术、社区和法律责任三方面的协同，仅有社区内部规则和审核机制不足以防范滥用行为。

Abstract: As AI-generated sexual content (AIG-SC) is increasingly produced, online communities have emerged to support creators' needs. To understand whether and how community governance attempts work to prevent abuse while supporting free expression, we interviewed 24 members and moderators of large AIG-SC online communities (10,000+ members) with stated rules against creating and sharing abusive content (e.g., AI-generated CSAM). Through in-depth interviews, we offer insight into: (1) how and why these communities form; (2) implicit community norms; (3) explicitly stated rules---and their operationalization via content moderation; and (4) tensions between community values and moderation that leave space for abusive behavior.
  Our findings reveal a complex picture: while many creators and communities have personal boundaries against abuse, advice and resources for creating any form of AI-generated sexual content are accessible to users regardless of their intentions. Further complicating community moderation are norms that center anti-censorship and non-judgment, which leave moderators to justify their actions using the limits of the law and terms of service. We end by reflecting on the ways in which technical, community, and legal governance may most effectively mitigate the production of abusive content.

</details>


### [101] [Weird Machines in Transport Layer Security](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13685)
*Michael Collins,Jada Cumberland,Brianne Dunn,Ross Gore,Samuel Jackson,Sachin Shetty,Jonathan Takeshita*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次将"诡异机器"（weird machine）理论扩展到TLS握手协议领域，证明OpenSSL和BoringSSL中合法的TLS原语（会话缓存、重协商逻辑、扩展解析、证书验证）可以组合成图灵完备的计算系统，并通过"信任驱动"（trust actuation）机制耦合到认证与信任决策。作者构建了两个实际演示：一个防御性的哨兵系统和一个认证绕过攻击，均无需内存破坏即可运作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前的诡异机器研究聚焦于软件系统（x86指令、ELF元数据、页表）和工控网络，但尚未有人系统性地探索TLS握手协议中隐含的计算能力。理解这些隐式计算能力对于发现新型安全漏洞和构建防御机制至关重要。

Method: 作者形式化了"信任驱动"概念，论证只要TLS实现提供会话存储、序列计数器上的算术运算、握手状态上的条件分支以及通过恢复或重试循环进行的迭代，就满足任意计算的充分条件。随后基于真实OpenSSL代码路径构建了两个演示系统（哨兵防御系统和认证绕过攻击），在Docker中对真实服务端和客户端二进制进行验证。

Result: 两个工作演示均成功运行：哨兵系统能够通过组合标准TLS原语检测异常握手行为；认证绕过攻击通过连接中重协商击败密码强度策略检查，且不涉及任何内存破坏或外部恶意软件。

Conclusion: TLS握手协议及其主导实现（OpenSSL、BoringSSL）中存在可被利用的隐式计算能力，其计算输出耦合到信任决策而非物理驱动。这为TLS安全研究开辟了新方向，表明即使没有内存漏洞，纯协议层面的逻辑组合也可能产生安全绕过。

Abstract: Weird machines are latent computational capabilities that emerge from the composition of architectural components. Prior work has studied this phenomenon extensively in software systems, including x86 instructions, ELF metadata, and page tables, and more recently in cyber-physical systems such as industrial control networks. This paper extends weird machine theory to a new domain: the Transport Layer Security (TLS) handshake and its two dominant implementations, OpenSSL and BoringSSL.
  We show that legitimate TLS primitives, including session cache entries, renegotiation logic, extension parsing, and certificate verification steps, compose into Turing-complete systems whose computation is coupled to authentication and trust decisions rather than physical actuation. We formalize this coupling, which we call trust actuation, and argue that any TLS implementation providing session storage, arithmetic on sequence counters, conditional branching on handshake state, and iteration through resumption or retry loops satisfies the conditions for arbitrary computation.
  We validate this theory with two working demonstrations built on real OpenSSL code paths. The first, a sentinel system, composes standard TLS primitives into a defensive mechanism that detects anomalous handshake behavior. The second, an authentication bypass, composes the same class of primitives into an attack that defeats a cipher-strength policy check through mid-connection renegotiation, without any memory corruption or external malware. Both demonstrations run against real server and client binaries in Docker.

</details>


### [102] [A Reproducibility Protocol for Cross-Implementation Evaluation of Post-Quantum ACVP Test Vectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13784)
*Christopher M. Frost*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文定义了一个产品中立、可复现的NIST ML-KEM实现验证协议（Protocol v2），对三个公开实现（@noble/post-quantum 0.7.0、liboqs 0.16.0、Go 1.26.4）在固定的ACVP语料库上进行了系统评测。结果显示所有1,650条可执行评估均与NIST预言机一致，510条不支持记录与Go能力边界吻合，成对重叠全部一致，但暴露了公共ML-KEM包在确定性和验证测试面存在实质性差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 加密标准的不同独立实现应产生一致的已知答案结果，但当语料库、修订版本、公共接口、排除项和证据未被精确定义时，一致性比较缺乏意义。作者旨在建立一种产品中立的可复现性协议，以严谨、透明且可验证的方式评估多个ML-KEM实现的一致性。

Method: 定义了Protocol v2：冻结供应商特定能力、对称应用单一验证错误分类学、保留每个被选案例、分离字节/验证判定/不支持操作/适配器错误。基于源码构建实验，在固定的公共ACVP语料库上对三个实现执行3次重复，覆盖2,160条基础记录的笛卡尔积。辅以三个冻结控制（字节比较、判定比较、畸形响应错误分离）和keyGen-ek投影诊断。

Result: 3次重复中所有1,650条可执行评估与NIST预言机匹配；510条不支持记录与Go预声明能力边界一致。noble-liboqs间720/720记录完全一致，每个Go配对210/210记录一致。keyGen-ek投影诊断匹配全部150条Go封装密钥投影。三个冻结控制均产生精确的预声明结果，无基础失败、适配器错误或状态不稳定。

Conclusion: 证据确立了有界可重复性，但揭示了实际标准缺口：公共ML-KEM包提供实质不同的确定性和验证测试面。研究不宣称认证、穷尽正确性、侧信道抗性、安全集成或生产保障；独立的第三方外部复现仍未观察到。

Abstract: Independent implementations of a cryptographic standard should reproduce the same known-answer results, yet agreement is meaningful only when the corpus, revisions, public interfaces, exclusions, and evidence are precisely stated. This study defines a product-neutral reproducibility protocol for three public implementations of NIST ML-KEM against a pinned public Automated Cryptographic Validation Protocol corpus. Protocol v2 freezes provider-specific capabilities, applies one validation-error taxonomy symmetrically, preserves every selected case, and separates bytes, validation verdicts, unsupported operations, and adapter errors. The source-built experiment evaluated @noble/post-quantum 0.7.0, liboqs 0.16.0, and Go 1.26.4. Across three repetitions, the required Cartesian product comprised 2,160 base records: all 1,650 declared executable evaluations matched the NIST oracle, and all 510 unsupported records matched Go's predeclared capability boundary. Pairwise agreement was complete on every executable overlap: 720 of 720 noble-liboqs records and 210 of 210 records for each Go pairing. A separate keyGen-ek-projection diagnostic matched all 150 Go encapsulation-key projections without counting them as full key generation. Three frozen controls independently exercised byte comparison, verdict comparison, and malformed-response error separation; each produced its exact predeclared outcome. No base failure, adapter error, or status instability occurred. The evidence establishes bounded author-run repeatability and exposes a practical standards gap: public ML-KEM packages provide materially different deterministic and validation-test surfaces. Independent external reproduction remains unobserved. The results do not establish certification, exhaustive correctness, side-channel resistance, secure integration, or production assurance.

</details>


### [103] [The ack3 H1 2026 DeFi Incident Dataset: Audit Scope Across 135 Security Incidents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13792)
*Josef Gattermayer,Jan Kalivoda,Arman Bašović*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文基于ack3公司H1 2026 DeFi事件数据集，分析135起DeFi安全事件的审计历史与事件路径范围。结果显示，在68起可识别审计历史的案件中，67.6%的攻击路径（占损失94.4%）超出了所有公开事前审计范围，表明"已审计"标签并不等同于项目级安全保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能合约审计通常只覆盖特定时间点的特定工件，但"已审计"标签常被误解为整个项目层面的安全保障。这种标签歧义可能导致用户和投资者对风险的误判，因此需要通过实证分析厘清审计历史与事件路径范围之间的关系。

Method: 使用ack3发布的H1 2026 DeFi事件数据集（覆盖2026年1月1日至6月29日），对135起DeFi安全事件中的审计历史与事件路径范围进行交叉分析；按事件路径是否落入公开事前审计范围进行分类，并结合损失金额进行加权分析；同时记录审计年龄、时间损失分布及受影响项目类型。

Result: 数据集报告总损失9.3986亿美元。68起事件可识别审计历史，其中46起攻击路径在全部公开事前审计范围之外，20起在至少一个范围内，2起无法确定。范围外路径占事件数67.6%、占报告损失94.4%。损失加权结果集中于两起大型事件，剔除后占比降至72.1%，但结论方向保持一致。

Conclusion: 项目级审计历史与事件路径范围是两个不同的变量，审计范围覆盖某一工件并不意味着覆盖攻击路径。该研究揭示了"已审计"标签的误导性，强调审计结果应当按范围而非按项目整体来解读。

Abstract: Smart-contract audits cover defined artifacts at a specific time, but the label audited is often treated as project-wide assurance. We analyze audit history and incident-path scope across 135 DeFi security incidents using the H1 2026 DeFi Incident Dataset published by cybersecurity company ack3 (https://ack3.ai), covering 1 January to 29 June 2026. The corpus reports USD 939.86 million in attributed loss. Audit history was identified for 68 incidents. Of these, 46 attack paths were outside all identified public pre-incident audit scopes, 20 were inside at least one scope, and 2 were unresolved. Within this 68-incident subset, outside-scope paths represented 67.6% by count and 94.4% of reported loss. The loss-weighted result was concentrated in two large incidents; excluding both reduced the share to 72.1%, while preserving the direction of the result. We also describe audit age, temporal loss distribution, and affected project types. The results show that project-level audit history and incident-path scope are distinct variables.

</details>


### [104] [Mobile Apps vs. Web Browsers: A User Perception Study with Android Apps and Google Chrome](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13803)
*Harel Berger*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本研究通过实验和问卷调查，考察Android智能手机用户在安全、隐私和可用性方面对移动应用与网页浏览器的感知差异，为开发者改进两大平台提供依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用户在选择移动应用还是网页浏览器访问在线服务时，会受到安全、隐私和可用性等多方面因素的影响，但目前对这些用户感知和偏好的研究不足，需要系统的实证调查来揭示关键关切和误解。

Method: 采用实验与问卷调查相结合的方法，以Android智能手机用户为研究对象，收集用户对移动应用与网页浏览器的安全、隐私和可用性感知数据。

Result: 研究发现用户在选择移动应用还是网页浏览器时存在特定的关切点和偏好，并揭示了用户可能存在的认知误区。

Conclusion: 研究结果可为开发者提供有价值的见解，帮助其通过解决用户关切和纠正认知误区，同时改进移动应用和网页浏览器的安全、隐私和可用性表现。

Abstract: This study examines user perceptions of mobile applications (apps) versus web browsers for accessing online services, with an emphasis on security, privacy, and usability aspects. Through a combination of an experiment and a survey with Android smartphone users, the research seeks to identify the key concerns and preferences that influence their choice between mobile apps and web browsers. The findings will offer valuable insights for developers to improve the security, privacy, and usability of both platforms by addressing user concerns and misconceptions.

</details>


### [105] [Characterizing the Variance Envelope: A Multi-Dimensional Analysis of Spectre Telemetry Across Architectures and Workloads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13920)
*Jaya Keshava Chandra Kotha,Jean-Luc Gaudiot*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统性地刻画了基于硬件性能计数器（HPC）的硬件攻击检测在不同架构（Intel、ARM、AMD）下的"方差包络"，证明静态检测模型在现实环境中因背景噪声、攻击变体和流量节奏等因素而失效，并提出需要架构感知的自适应监控方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于HPC和机器学习的硬件攻击检测方法仅在受控环境中有效，静态模型在面对真实世界的背景噪声、多种攻击变体和对抗性流量节奏时性能大幅下降。为弥合这一差距，亟需系统化地理解攻击签名在跨架构条件下的变化范围。

Method: 作者在Intel、ARM和AMD三种架构上构建了包含三种攻击变体、四种流量节奏模式和四种背景噪声条件的实验矩阵，刻画攻击签名随执行环境变化的"方差包络"（variance envelope），并针对各架构进行微架构层面的逐项分析。

Result: 分析证明HPC签名高度脆弱，极易受执行环境影响而变形；此外发现AMD Jaguar上Prime+Probe攻击存在硬件级别的持续失效，揭示了严重的微架构瓶颈。

Conclusion: 可靠的运行时硬件攻击检测不能依赖静态模型，必须采用架构感知的自适应监控策略，才能应对现实环境中的多样性和不确定性。

Abstract: Hardware attacks like Spectre exploit built-in processor vulnerabilities, leaving anomalous footprints in Hardware Performance Counter (HPC) metrics. While machine learning can detect these footprints in controlled settings, static models fail in the real world when confronted with background system noise, diverse attack variants, and adversarial traffic pacing. To close this gap, this paper characterizes the "variance envelope"-the full range of how attack signatures shift- across Intel, ARM, and AMD architectures. We evaluate an extensive experimental matrix encompassing three attack variants, four pacing modes, and four background-noise conditions. Our analysis proves that HPC signatures are highly fragile and easily warped by their execution environment. Furthermore, we expose a critical microarchitectural bottleneck: the persistent, hardware-level failure of Prime+Probe attacks on the AMD Jaguar. Ultimately, this comprehensive characterization demonstrates that reliable runtime detection requires architecture-aware, adaptive monitoring rather than static models.

</details>


### [106] [Vaulted Passkeys: A Device-Bound Proposal for Authenticated Credential Export and Import](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13806)
*Pol Henarejos*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出Vaulted Passkeys架构，通过256位随机密钥Kvault和HKDF分离密钥及四种AEAD协议，在硬件认证器间实现受保护的凭证状态导出与恢复，解决设备绑定凭证的灾备恢复缺口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硬件认证器刻意抵抗私钥提取，但更换设备、灾难恢复和受控迁移需要凭证可移植性。现有做法（预先注册额外认证器）只是冗余而非原始凭证的备份，存在恢复缺口。

Method: 提出Vaulted Passkeys设备绑定架构：256位随机Kvault通过HKDF分离密钥保护PKV1凭证信封，使用四种显式AEAD配置；设计分离注册与导出/导入流程，以及必需的金库与可选的身份；包含角色分离系统模型、线格式、威胁分析和可证伪评估计划。

Result: 原型实现证明了可行性，但作者明确声明其非正式安全证明，也非提议的最终标准。

Conclusion: 该架构为设备绑定凭证提供了不克隆完整认证器、不向日常桌面软件暴露明文私钥的受保护可移植性方案，填补了现有冗余注册方式无法覆盖的恢复缺口，但尚需正式安全验证和标准化流程。

Abstract: Hardware authenticators deliberately resist private-key extraction, yet replacement, disaster recovery, and controlled migration create a legitimate need for portability. Existing guidance for device-bound credentials commonly reduces recovery risk by registering an additional authenticator before failure. That creates an independent credential registration and requires replacement hardware to exist in advance; it is redundancy, not a backup of the original credential. This paper addresses the resulting recovery gap by exporting protected credential state while the source is available and restoring it to hardware acquired later, without cloning a complete authenticator or exposing plaintext private keys to routine desktop software. We propose Vaulted Passkeys, a device-bound architecture in which a random 256-bit Kvault protects authenticated PKV1 credential envelopes through HKDF-separated keys and four explicit AEAD profiles. The design separates enrollment from export/import and the required vault from optional identity. We contribute a role-separated system model, wire format, threat analysis, implementation mapping, and falsifiable evaluation plan. The prototype demonstrates feasibility but is neither a formal security proof nor a proposed final standard.

</details>


### [107] [TLF: Rapid Characterization of RF Transceiver Parameters in Embedded Systems via Bus-Level Interception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13815)
*Larry Hernandez,Sergey Bratus*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出TLF（收发器电梯框架），一种从微控制器与收发器IC之间的总线级迹线中恢复RF收发器配置和运行时行为的工具。通过状态化协议解码器重建RF参数，并能恢复硬件加密密钥、跳频表及序列，在SX1233和SX1276两个平台验证，可在数秒内从单次总线捕获中恢复完整RF配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硬件逆向工程中，RF收发器作为嵌入式系统的关键组件，其配置和运行时行为对目标分析至关重要。然而现有的逆向工具通常聚焦于固件层面的分析和软件协议的恢复，忽略了芯片间总线级通信中蕴含的transceiver配置信息。需要一个自动化的工具来从总线迹线中恢复收发器的完整配置，以支持匹配接收机配置或制定针对性对抗措施。

Method: TLF包含两层核心设计：(1) 状态化协议解码器——针对具体transceiver的寄存器和数据接口构建，通过拦截寄存器写入和FIFO传输重建工作RF参数和运行时行为；(2) 可插拔协议解码器——在上述芯片层之上解释恢复的FIFO载荷作为应用PDU。对于硬件辅助跳频或固件自定义跳频调度的场景，解码器还可提取信道表、跳频序列和时序信息。

Result: 在两个不同Semtech家族的平台上评估：基于SX1233的UAV C2调制解调器（固件级FHSS，逐包同步字轮换）和基于SX1276的Meshtastic节点（LoRa寄存器覆盖层）。单次总线捕获在数秒内即可恢复完整寄存器暴露的RF配置（调制、频段规划、相位行为），无需预先了解目标固件，并在Meshtastic上完成了端到端的应用PDU解码演示。

Conclusion: TLF证明了从总线级迹线中以高保真度恢复RF收发器配置的可行性，其恢复深度受限于transceiver的寄存器接口——纯固件实现的参数（自定义FEC、白化、加密）只能以不透明FIFO载荷的形式观测。该方法需要物理访问或仿真目标硬件。

Abstract: We present TLF (Transceiver Lifter Framework), a tool for recovering RF transceiver configuration and runtime behavior from bus-level traces captured between a microcontroller and its transceiver IC. A stateful protocol decoder, built against the transceiver's register and data interface, reconstructs operating RF parameters and behavior from intercepted register writes and FIFO transfers. For bus-attached transceivers whose hardware-cryptography keys are loaded through the intercepted host interface, key material is also recoverable. Where the firmware drives frequency hopping -- either through a hardware-assisted engine or a custom schedule -- the decoder extracts the channel table, hop sequence, and timing. We evaluate the approach on two targets from different Semtech families: an SX1233-based UAV C2 modem employing firmware-level FHSS with per-packet sync word rotation, and an SX1276-based Meshtastic node exercising the LoRa register overlay. From a single bus capture, processed in seconds, TLF recovers the complete register-exposed RF configuration (modulation, band plan, phase behavior) without prior knowledge of the target firmware -- sufficient to configure a matched receiver or develop targeted countermeasures. Above the chip layer, a pluggable protocol decoder interprets recovered FIFO payloads as application PDUs, demonstrated end-to-end on Meshtastic. Firmware-level cryptographic state remains, as expected, opaque. The approach requires physical access or emulation of the target hardware, and its recovery depth is bounded by the transceiver's register interface: parameters implemented entirely in firmware (custom FEC, whitening, encryption) are observable only as opaque FIFO payloads.

</details>


### [108] [Verified Pythagorean Composition for Adaptive Cryptographic Games: Noise Flooding in Homomorphic Encryption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13846)
*Yi Lee,Alexandru Cojocaru,Junyi Liu,Xiaodi Wu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文用Rocq和SSProve对近似同态加密的噪声淹没（noise flooding）防御的安全性证明进行了机器验证，形式化了q-查询IND-CPAD安全性约简，避免了普通混合论证中因多次混合而导致的线性损失，而只产生一次平方根损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 噪声淹没是同态加密抵抗解密攻击的标准防御手段，但其安全性证明对组合（composition）异常敏感。直接用统计接近模拟替换每个自适应解密回答并做普通混合论证会线性损失q；密码学证明需要累积条件KL散度成本并仅最终转换一次为统计距离，从而获得参数关键的平方根损失。这种精细的论证需要机器验证来确保其正确性。

Method: 在Rocq和SSProve框架下形式化安全约简。针对任意满足近似正确性和IND-CPA安全的全同态加密方案，为每个q-查询IND-CPAD敌手构造约简。其核心是建立一个新的基于SSProve语义的关系程序逻辑，通过"Pythagorean judgment"组合条件KL预算而不转换为统计距离，并利用一个经过验证的迹编译器将局部预言机规则提升到任意自适应程序，仅在最后做一次转换。

Result: 形式化证明了安全性界：Pr[IND-CPAD_NF^A=1] ≤ β_CPA(B_{A,q}) + √(qn)/(2γ)，其中n是明文维度，γ是淹没宽度乘数。该界反映了仅一次的平方根损失而非q的线性损失。

Conclusion: 通过机器验证的形式化方法，成功证明了噪声淹没防御在q-查询自适应场景下具有参数最优的安全性界，验证了密码学证明中先积累KL成本再单次转换策略的正确性，为近似同态加密的安全性提供了可信的形式化保证。

Abstract: Noise flooding is a standard defense against decryption attacks on approximate homomorphic encryption, but its security proof is unusually sensitive to composition. Replacing each of $q$ adaptive decryption answers with a statistically close simulation and applying an ordinary hybrid argument loses linearly in $q$. The cryptographic proof instead accumulates conditional Kullback-Leibler (KL) costs and converts to statistical distance once, giving the parameter-critical square-root loss.
  We machine-check this argument using Rocq and SSProve. Given any fully homomorphic encryption scheme that is approximately correct and IND-CPA secure, we formalize a reduction for every $q$-query IND-CPAD adversary and prove \[
  \Pr[\mathsf{IND\text{-}CPAD}_{\mathsf{NF}}^{\mathcal A}=1]
  \leq β_{\mathsf{CPA}}(\mathcal B_{\mathcal A,q})
  + \frac{\sqrt{qn}}{2γ}. \] where $n$ is the plaintext dimension and $γ$ is the flooding-width multiplier. Our proof constructs a new relational program logic over SSProve semantics. Its Pythagorean judgment composes conditional KL budgets without converting them to statistical distance, and a verified trace compiler lifts a local oracle rule to arbitrary adaptive programs with a single final conversion.

</details>


### [109] [CipherSight: Robust Website Fingerprinting via Record-Resource Semantic Supervision under Distribution Shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13905)
*Runhan Song,Qiqi Liu,Chuanzhou Pan,Zhenquan Ding,Youquan Xian,Chongru Fan,Lei Cui,Wei Wang,Zhiyu Hao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: CipherSight是一个基于TLS记录的分层框架，用于鲁棒的HTTPS网站指纹识别，通过联合编码多个记录级属性、捕获流内依赖和流间交互，并采用掩码记录建模和语义蒸馏，在闭集和开放环境下均显著优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有HTTPS网站指纹识别方法主要从原始TCP包序列中学习，对传输层伪影敏感，难以捕获稳定且泛化的网站表征，导致在时序漂移、地理漂移和未见网站等实际场景下性能显著下降。

Method: CipherSight采用分层架构，从TLS记录而非TCP包序列中学习网站表征：(1) 联合编码多个记录级属性；(2) 分层捕获流内TLS记录间的依赖关系和跨流交互模式；(3) 引入掩码记录建模(MRM)任务学习上下文流量语义；(4) 利用细粒度记录-资源标注作为特权监督，通过结构感知目标和语义蒸馏增强表征鲁棒性。

Result: 在超过2000个网站类别的闭集场景下达到95.41%的准确率；在时序漂移和地理漂移条件下保持90%以上准确率，在所有评估基线上实现一致的优势。

Conclusion: CipherSight验证了从TLS记录层而非传输层进行网站指纹识别的有效性，通过分层建模和语义蒸馏显著提升了模型对分布漂移和未知网站的鲁棒性，为实际场景下的HTTPS网站指纹识别提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: HTTPS website fingerprinting (WF) aims to identify visited websites from metadata observable in encrypted traffic. However, real-world deployments introduce a significant out-of-distribution (OOD) problem caused by temporal and geographic changes, while previously unseen websites are common in open-world scenarios. Existing methods primarily learn from raw TCP packet sequences and struggle to capture stable and generalizable website representations, resulting in performance degradation under practical conditions.
  We propose CipherSight, a TLS-record-based hierarchical framework for robust HTTPS WF. Unlike existing approaches that rely on TCP packet sequences and are sensitive to transport-layer artifacts, CipherSight learns website representations from TLS records by jointly encoding multiple record-level attributes. It introduces a hierarchical architecture that captures both intra-flow dependencies among TLS records and inter-flow interactions across concurrent flows, enabling the model to exploit structural patterns in HTTPS traffic. Besides, to learn robust representations, CipherSight employs a masked record modeling (MRM) task to capture contextual traffic semantics and leverages fine-grained record-resource annotations as privileged supervision through structure-aware objectives and semantic distillation. Experiments show that CipherSight achieves 95.41% accuracy across more than 2,000 website classes in the closed-world setting and maintains over 90% accuracy under both temporal and geographic drift, consistently outperforming all evaluated baselines.

</details>


### [110] [CoSA: Context-Aware Severity Assessment via Context Analysis with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13928)
*Jinfeng Jiang,Yikun Li,Chengran Yang,Ting Zhang,Wen Bin Leow,Yide Yin,Eng Lieh Ouh,Lwin Khin Shar,David Lo*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出CoSA方法，通过构建代码属性图和两阶段仓库级上下文筛选策略，利用agentic LLM检索并汇总CVSS相关证据，由轻量级transformer预测CVSS基础指标。在包含90种CWE类型、6,816个标注实例的仓库级数据集上，相较最佳基线提升14.4%准确率和15.3% Macro-F1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CVSS基础指标的人工评估耗时费力，而现有自动化方法往往缺乏对仓库级证据的捕捉能力。CVSS许多基础指标的评估需要仓库范围内的上下文信息，但相关证据分散在整个仓库中且伴随大量噪声，导致难以有效提取。

Method: CoSA包含三个核心组件：(1) 构建代码属性图（CPG）作为仓库的结构化表示；(2) 两阶段仓库剪枝策略——先进行轻量级静态剪枝保留结构邻近上下文，再通过agentic LLM引导的剪枝保留CVSS相关上下文并收集支持性证据；(3) 将检索到的仓库上下文汇总为紧凑的、按CVSS指标划分的文本摘要，输入轻量级transformer预测器生成各基础指标的评估结果。此外，作者构建了一个包含6,816个CVSS标注实例、覆盖90种CWE类型的高质量仓库级数据集。

Result: 在真实漏洞上的实验表明，CoSA一致性地优于函数级基线和纯LLM基线，预测准确率提升14.4%，Macro-F1提升15.3%。结果表明显式的、面向指标维度的仓库上下文检索对实用且可靠的自动化严重性评估至关重要。

Conclusion: CoSA验证了仓库级上下文感知对CVSS自动化评估的关键价值，通过结构化图谱剪枝与agentic LLM的结合，有效克服了仓库中证据分散和噪声干扰的问题，为实际漏洞严重性评估提供了一种可靠且高效的自动化方案。

Abstract: Accurate vulnerability severity assessment is essential for prioritizing remediation, yet manually assessing Common Vulnerability Scoring System (CVSS) base metrics remains labor-intensive. Existing automated approaches often fail to capture the repository-level evidence required for assessing many CVSS base metrics. Such repository-aware assessment is challenging because relevant evidence is scattered across the entire repository under heavy noise.
  To address these challenges, we present CoSA, a Context-aware vulnerability Severity Assessment approach that infers CVSS base metrics from repository artifacts. CoSA constructs a code property graph (CPG) and applies a two-stage repository-pruning strategy: lightweight static pruning to preserve structurally proximal context, followed by an agentic large language model (LLM)-guided pruning step to retain CVSS-relevant context while collecting supporting evidence. The LLM then consolidates the retrieved repository context into compact, CVSS metric-wise textual summaries, which are fed into a lightweight transformer predictor. We also construct a higher-quality repository-level dataset comprising 6,816 CVSS labeled instances spanning 90 Common Weakness Enumeration (CWE) types.
  Experiments on real-world vulnerabilities show that CoSA consistently outperforms function-level and pure-LLM baselines. It improves prediction accuracy by 14.4% and Macro-F1 by 15.3% over the best-performing baseline, suggesting that explicit, metric-oriented repository context retrieval is crucial for practical and reliable automated severity assessment.

</details>


### [111] [Extracting and Verifying Illicit Bitcoin Addresses from Underground Forum Discussions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13930)
*Abdoul Nasser Hassane Amadou,Arnaud Legout,Imane Fouad,Konstantin Avrachenkov,Anas Motii*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种可复现的、基于证据的比特币标签构建流水线。该流水线结合LLM辅助筛选、专家审查和链上验证，从HackForums地下论坛的十五年存档中提取与非法交易明确关联的比特币地址，最终发布了包含2,438个手动验证的非法比特币地址数据集，涵盖2010-2024年间的12类网络犯罪类型，并公开了完整提取流水线以支持可复现研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有比特币标签数据集大多来源于社区举报、区块链启发式规则、事件特定收集或专有标注流程，其构建方法难以公开复现，且往往缺乏地址直接参与非法活动的充分证据，阻碍了加密货币网络犯罪研究的可验证性和可重复性。

Method: 构建了一条可复现的三阶段流水线：(1) 利用LLM对HackForums论坛存档中的讨论进行预筛选，识别与非法交易相关的比特币地址；(2) 由领域专家对筛选结果进行审查，确保标签与论坛讨论上下文匹配；(3) 通过区块链上的链上验证确认地址确实参与了所讨论的非法交易。每个发布的标签均附带论坛讨论上下文证据和链上验证结果。

Result: 产出了包含2,438个手动验证的非法比特币地址数据集，时间覆盖2010至2024年，涉及12类网络犯罪类别。数据集、时间元数据及完整提取流水线均已公开发布。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了第一个从地下论坛语料中系统构建、具备双重证据（论坛上下文+链上验证）且完全可复现的比特币非法地址数据集，为加密货币便利化网络犯罪的研究提供了更可信的数据基础。

Abstract: Existing labeled Bitcoin datasets are largely derived from community-reported abuse, blockchain heuristics, incident-specific collections, or proprietary labeling processes. Their construction methods are rarely publicly reproducible and often provide limited evidence that an address was directly involved in illicit activity. We present a reproducible pipeline for constructing evidence-backed Bitcoin labels from HackForums, an underground cybercrime forum with fifteen years of archived activity. The pipeline combines LLM-assisted screening, expert review, and on-chain validation to identify Bitcoin addresses explicitly associated with illicit transactions discussed on the forum. Each released label is supported by contextual evidence from underground discussions and validated on-chain. The resulting dataset contains 2,438 manually verified illicit Bitcoin addresses spanning 2010-2024 and twelve cybercrime categories assigned during LLM screening. We release the dataset, temporal metadata, and the complete extraction pipeline to support reproducible research on cryptocurrency-facilitated cybercrime.

</details>


### [112] [Vectorized SQIsign Implementation Using AVX-512](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13948)
*Weize Wang,Chutong Wang,Yu Wu,Qifan Xue,Jieyu Zheng,Yunlei Zhao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次对SQIsign（唯一的基于同源的NIST后量子签名方案）进行了基于AVX-512 IFMA指令集的全面向量化实现，通过系统重构整个计算栈（从素域/扩域算术到椭圆曲线运算和配对计算），在NIST安全级别I下实现了签名2.69倍和验证3.18倍的加速。同时回应了AVX-512过时的误解，指出AVX10架构将标准化这些指令，确保长期可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SQIsign是NIST后量子密码标准化过程中唯一的基于同源的数字签名方案，拥有紧凑的密钥和签名尺寸，但其签名过程计算密集，阻碍了实际部署。已有的优化多集中在算法层面，SIMD向量化这一关键优化维度尚未被充分探索。

Method: 利用AVX-512整数融合乘加（IFMA）指令集，对SQIsign计算栈进行系统性重新设计，涵盖：素域和扩域算术、椭圆曲线操作（批量点加倍和标量乘法）、基于立方算术的配对计算及二维同源求值。与Qlapoti技术相结合，并论证了向量化策略的架构无关性与AVX10的未来兼容性。

Result: 与参考实现相比，在NIST安全级别I下，签名提速2.69倍，验证提速3.18倍。该实现是SQIsign首个全面的向量化实现。

Conclusion: SIMD向量化是同源密码这一后量子方案中被低估的重要优化维度，独立于算法创新即可带来显著加速。向量化策略具有架构通用性，可为更广泛的同源密码构造提供方法论基础；配合AVX10的标准化部署，这些优化具有长期有效性。

Abstract: SQIsign is the sole isogeny-based digital signature scheme submitted to the NIST Post-Quantum Cryptography standardization process, distinguished by its foundation on the hardness of the endomorphism ring problem for supersingular elliptic curves. While offering compact key and signature sizes, SQIsign's practical deployment is hindered by computationally intensive signing procedures. This paper presents the first comprehensive vectorized implementation of SQIsign utilizing the AVX-512 Integer Fused Multiply-Add (IFMA) instruction set architecture. By systematically redesigning the computational stack---encompassing prime-field and extension-field arithmetic, elliptic curve operations including batched point doubling and scalar multiplication, as well as pairing computations via cubical arithmetic and two-dimensional isogeny evaluations---we achieve substantial performance improvements over the reference implementation. When combined with Qlapoti technology, our implementation attains a $2.69\times$ speedup for signing and a $3.18\times$ improvement for verification at NIST security level I.
  Contrary to misconceptions regarding the obsolescence of AVX-512, we emphasize that Intel's AVX10 instruction set architecture (revision 10.2, scheduled for widespread deployment in late 2026) will standardize AVX-512 capabilities---including IFMA instructions---across both performance and efficiency cores, ensuring long-term viability of these optimization techniques. Furthermore, our vectorization strategies are architecture-agnostic and provide a methodological foundation applicable to broader isogeny-based cryptographic constructions. This work demonstrates that SIMD vectorization represents a critical yet underexplored optimization dimension for post-quantum isogeny-based schemes, independent of recent algorithmic advances.

</details>


### [113] [Structural Leakage in Graph Encryption: Attacks and Defenses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13981)
*Hua Shen,Renzhi Chen,Ge Wu,Willy Susilo,Jing Chen,Mingwu Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了图加密方案（GES）中单对最短路径（SPSP）查询的结构泄漏问题，融合了两项近期工作：首先分析了PathGES方案在重轻分解和规范片段编码下的结构弱点，发现超过99%的令牌-路径映射为一对一映射，容易被Falzon-Paterson攻击和路径长度侧信道推断攻击；其次提出了Fragment Tree攻击利用这些结构弱点实现最高10.24%的查询内容精确恢复；最后提出了BlindGES增强方案，通过Merge-and-Divide机制和两级多映射索引将一对一映射降至20%以下，设置时间减少50%，存储开销降低32%，路径长度泄漏控制在1%以内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图加密方案虽然支持安全外包图数据和高效查询，但存在结构性泄漏问题。特别是SPSP查询方案中，重轻分解可能导致大量令牌-路径一对一映射，使得攻击者能够恢复查询内容并从旁路推断路径长度，严重威胁方案的安全性。

Method: 本文采用了多管齐下的方法：(1)对PathGES方案进行安全分析，识别其HLD分解中的不平衡性和令牌-路径映射结构；(2)设计Fragment Tree攻击利用这些结构弱点恢复查询内容；(3)提出BlindGES增强方案，引入Merge-and-Divide机制和两级多映射索引来消除一对一映射并限制路径长度泄漏；(4)在七个真实数据集上进行实验评估。

Result: 分析揭示了PathGES在真实数据集上超过99%的令牌-路径映射为一对一映射；提出的Fragment Tree攻击在稀疏图上实现了最高10.24%的精确恢复率；BlindGES将一对一映射降至20%以下，设置时间减少50%，存储开销降低32%，路径长度泄漏控制在1%以内。

Conclusion: 该报告系统性地呈现了攻击方法论、防御机制、安全证明及实验评估，表明现有的H LD分解和规范片段编码方法在SPSP查询中具有显著的结构泄漏风险，而BlindGES通过Merge-and-Divide机制和两级索引设计能够有效缓解这些泄漏，为图加密方案的安全性提升提供了可靠的改进方向。

Abstract: Graph encryption schemes (GES) enable secure outsourcing of graph data while supporting efficient queries. This report provides a comprehensive analysis of structural leakage in GES for single-pair shortest path (SPSP) queries, integrating findings from two recent works. First, we analyze PathGES, a scheme designed to resist query recovery attacks through heavy-light decomposition (HLD) and canonical fragment encoding. Our analysis reveals that PathGES suffers from significant imbalances in HLD decomposition, with over 99% of token-path mappings being one-to-one on real-world datasets, enabling both the Falzon-Paterson attack and side-channel inference of path lengths. Second, we present Fragment Tree attack that exploits these structural weaknesses to recover query contents, achieving up to 10.24% exact recovery on sparse graphs. Third, we introduce BlindGES, an enhanced scheme incorporating a Merge-and-Divide mechanism and two-level multimap index that reduces one-to-one mappings to below 20%, cuts setup time by 50%, reduces storage overhead by 32%, and limits path length leakage to under 1%. This report systematically presents attack methodologies, defense mechanisms, security proofs, and experimental evaluations on seven real-world datasets.

</details>


### [114] [P2Skill: Privacy Preserving Skill Distillation for Cloud-Local LLM Inference Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14094)
*Myunghoon Ryu,Geunpyo Park,Sungjoon Lee,XinYu Piao,Jong-Kook Kim*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: P2Skill是一种基于提示词的技能蒸馏方法，通过本地小语言模型（SLM）自主执行分解、PII感知路由、改写和重构，由云端大语言模型（LLM）从执行失败中迭代精炼技能，无需隐私专用微调或辅助检测器，在四领域基准上隐私保护推理质量比基线提升1.69倍和3.66倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云端-本地LLM推理系统需要在利用云端大模型推理能力的同时保护个人设备上的敏感数据。现有隐私保护方法（提示扰动、实体掩码、模型微调）可能扭曲上下文语义或需要额外训练，缺乏无需额外训练且保持语义完整性的方法。

Method: 提出P2Skill方法：本地SLM通过遵循技能提示词（skill prompts）自主完成四步操作——分解（decomposition）、PII感知路由（PII-aware routing）、改写（paraphrasing）和重构（reconstruction）。技能由云端LLM从执行失败中迭代精炼（iteratively refined from execution failures），使本地SLM能够泛化超越记忆化的PII模式，无需隐私专用微调或学习型辅助检测器。

Result: 在四领域基准（four-domain benchmark）上的评估表明，P2Skill相比先前基线在隐私保留推理质量上分别取得了1.69倍和3.66倍的提升。

Conclusion: P2Skill通过提示驱动的技能蒸馏，实现了无需额外训练即可在本地SLM上完成PII保护推理，克服了传统方法需要微调或额外检测器的局限性，显著提升了隐私保留推理质量。

Abstract: Cloud-local LLM inference systems have the potential to use the reasoning capability of large cloud models while protecting sensitive user data on personal devices. Cloud-bound requests must exclude personally identifiable information (PII) to prevent external data leakage. Existing privacy-preserving methods rely on prompt perturbation, entity masking, or model fine-tuning, but these approaches may distort contextual semantics or require additional training. This paper proposes P2Skill, a prompt-based skill distillation method in which a local small language model (SLM) autonomously performs decomposition, PII-aware routing, paraphrasing, and reconstruction by following the skill prompts. Skills are iteratively refined from execution failures by a cloud LLM, enabling the local SLM to generalize beyond memorized PII patterns, and therefore P2Skill requires no privacy-specific fine-tuning or learned auxiliary detectors. Evaluation on a four-domain benchmark shows that P2Skill achieves $1.69\times$ and $3.66\times$ higher privacy-preserved inference quality than previous baselines.

</details>


### [115] [BGA: A noise-immune neural distillation framework for malicious signature extraction in high-entropy encrypted flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14126)
*Sheng Hong,Yixuan Huang,Weiwei Jiang,Junyuan Zhang,Jiacheng Wang,Ruijian Jiao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出BGA框架，一种抗噪声的神经蒸馏框架，用于缓解高熵TLS 1.3流量中的注意力稀释问题，通过ANOVA特征解耦、WGAN-GP少数类合成以及BiLSTM与自适应门控多头注意力机制，在CIC-IDS-2018和Edge-IIoT基准上实现超过95.2%的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TLS 1.3加密流量的高熵特性导致注意力机制被稀释，难以有效检测加密威胁情报中的恶意行为，需要一种能够抗噪声的检测框架来解决这一问题。

Method: 采用三阶段方法：(1) ANOVA方差分析将高判别性的控制平面特征（工业设定点）与随机加密噪声解耦；(2) 集成WGAN-GP模块（施加1-Lipschitz约束）合成高保真少数类样本来解决86,878条流记录中的极端类不平衡；(3) 核心架构结合BiLSTM提取时间依赖和自适应门控多头注意力机制，作为神经滤波器动态抑制加密伪影、放大恶意签名。

Result: 在CIC-IDS-2018和Edge-IIoT基准上所有关键指标超过95.2%；罕见恶意状态命令注入（MSCI）攻击的检测召回率提升43.2%；噪声注入压力测试表明比vanilla Transformer高8.57%的结构韧性；推理延迟低至0.2820毫秒（ARM上理论扩展估计为1.6920毫秒）。

Conclusion: BGA框架在高熵TLS 1.3流量中展现出优越的检测性能和结构韧性，极低推理延迟表明其在异构工业边缘网关上具有实时可行的潜力，为未来硬件实现提供了有前景的架构基线。

Abstract: To mitigate attention dilution in high-entropy TLS 1.3 flows, we propose BGA, a noise-immune neural distillation framework for encrypted threat intelligence.The methodology first employs Analysis of Variance (ANOVA) to decouple high-discriminatory control-plane features - specifically industrial setpoints - from stochastic cryptographic noise. To resolve the extreme class imbalance within a corpus of 86,878 flow records, a Wasserstein GAN with Gradient Penalty (WGAN-GP) module, enforcing the 1-Lipschitz constraint, is integrated to synthesize high-fidelity minority samples, elevating the detection recall of rare Malicious State Command Injections(MSCI) attacks by 43.2%. At its core, the BGA architecture integrates Bidirectional Long Short-Term Memory (BiLSTM) for temporal dependency extraction and an Adaptive Gated Multi-Head Attention mechanism. This gated unit functions as a neural filter to dynamically suppress encryption artifacts while amplifying malicious signatures. Extensive evaluations on CIC-IDS-2018 and Edge-IIoT benchmarks demonstrate a performance ceiling exceeding 95.2% across all key metrics. Furthermore, noise-injection stress tests confirm BGAs superior structural resilience with a 8.57% performance margin over vanilla Transformers, while its ultra-low inference latency of 0.2820 ms (estimated 1.6920 ms via theoretical scaling for ARM) indicates a high potential for real-time feasibility on heterogeneous industrial edge gateways, providing a promising architectural baseline for future hardware implementation.

</details>


### [116] [MazeRunner: Nonlinear Task and Clue Orchestration for LLM-driven Black-Box Automated Penetration Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14216)
*Zhenyuan Li,Yi Jiang,Junjie Cheng,Yaokun Li,Jing Qiu,Shouling Ji*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: MazeRunner是一个基于三智能体任务与线索编排框架的自主渗透测试系统，专门解决真实黑盒测试中的非线性攻击图推断、失败误判和长距离线索关联等问题。在10个HTB目标上，MazeRunner完成47.7%的标注子任务，显著优于PentestGPT-V2和Claude Code基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的渗透测试代理主要在线性简化场景中执行端到端工作流，而真实黑盒测试本质上是非线性的：攻击图初始未知、需从环境反馈中增量推断；观测可能产生多个攻击分支、失败具有歧义性、关键线索可能跨越很长的行动时间跨度。现有代理因此容易陷入深度优先探索陷阱、误诊失败原因并遗忘先前的证据。

Method: MazeRunner采用三智能体任务与线索编排框架：全局编排（global orchestration）负责宏观策略与分支管理，上下文密集型执行（context-intensive execution）处理具体攻击操作，失败导向审查（failure-oriented review）进行错误诊断与修复。系统通过持久维护任务状态和环境证据，支持动作修订、前置条件恢复、分支切换和长距离线索关联。

Result: 在10个近期发布的HTB目标上、每次限20M LLM token且防止目标特定方案泄漏的条件下，MazeRunner完成47.7%的标注子任务（PentestGPT-V2为36.2%，Claude Code为34.2%），在6个目标上达成用户级或更高访问权限（其中2个获得root权限），而基线仅在2个目标上达到用户级且从未获得root权限。执行轨迹分析显示MazeRunner探索更多攻击分支并以更高效率获取shell。

Conclusion: 通过分离编排、执行和审查职责并持久维护任务状态与证据，三智能体框架能够显著提升LLM代理在真实黑盒渗透测试中的非线性推理能力，有效缓解深度优先陷阱、失败误判和长距离线索遗忘问题。

Abstract: Penetration testing is essential yet resource-intensive. Although large language models (LLMs) show promise for automating security auditing, existing agents mainly execute end-to-end workflows in simplified linear scenarios. Real-world black-box testing is fundamentally nonlinear: the attack graph is initially unknown and must be incrementally inferred from environmental feedback. Observations may reveal multiple attack branches, failures are often ambiguous, and critical clues may span long action horizons. Existing agents therefore tend to become trapped in depth-first exploration, misdiagnose failures, and forget prior evidence. We present MazeRunner, an autonomous penetration testing system built on a three-agent task-and-clue orchestration framework. It separates global orchestration, context-intensive execution, and failure-oriented review while persistently maintaining task states and environmental evidence. This design supports action revision, prerequisite recovery, branch switching, and long-range clue correlation. We evaluate MazeRunner on 10 recently released HTB targets, limiting each system-target run to 20 million LLM tokens and preventing target-specific solution leakage. With Claude Sonnet 4.5, MazeRunner completes 47.7% of annotated subtasks, compared with 36.2% for PentestGPT-V2 and 34.2% for Claude Code. It achieves user-level or higher access on six targets, including root access on two; each same-model baseline reaches user-level access on only two targets and never obtains root access. Execution-trace analysis further shows that MazeRunner explores more attack branches and acquires shells more efficiently.

</details>


### [117] [A Four-Axis Trustworthiness Benchmark for LLM-as-Judge in Principle-Based Regulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14329)
*Dipankar Sarkar*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出原则性监管中的LLM法官必须从四个维度评估：准确性、释义鲁棒性、对抗鲁棒性和校准性。发布Principle-Bench基准（168个加密货币金融推广场景映射到两个英国FCA原则），引入Ceca（校准示例聚类评估器），发现没有任何方法在所有轴上占优，120B LLM法官在对抗性输入上表现崩溃。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原则性监管使用"公平、清晰、不误导"等评价性标准，无法简化为二元谓词，越来越多地使用LLM-as-judge替代。但缺乏对这类法官进行全面系统评估的基准，现有方法只关注单一维度（如准确率），忽视了对抗鲁棒性、释义鲁棒性和校准性。

Method: 构建Principle-Bench基准：168个加密资产金融推广场景，映射到英国FCA两条原则，包含释义、对抗性关键词堆砌和边界扰动三类扰动（基于预注册规则编写）。引入Ceca（Calibrated Exemplar-Cluster Assessment），一种校准且可审计的评估器，输出每个示例精确的反事实归因。评估了关键词计数、三种sentence-transformer嵌入器、开源权重LLM法官和校准级联。

Result: 没有任何方法在所有四个轴上占优。120B LLM法官在良性输入上最强，但在关键词堆砌的Consumer Duty输入上准确率从0.74暴跌至0.27（损失47个准确率点），表现为"合规剧场"。另一模型家族的法官在该子集上Cohen's kappa仅为0.16，表明失败源于模型本身而非语料。部署级原则性监管LLM法官必须报告逐原则的对抗欺骗率和事后校准。

Conclusion: 任何用于原则性监管的部署级LLM法官，在报告总体准确率之外，必须同时报告逐原则的对抗欺骗性与事后校准指标。现状表明当前LLM法官在对抗性输入下严重不可靠，需建立更全面的评估标准。

Abstract: Principle-based regulation, with evaluative standards such as "fair, clear, and not misleading" or "deliver good outcomes", cannot be reduced to binary predicates, and LLM-as-judge is increasingly used as the substitute. Our position is that any such judge must be evaluated on four axes: accuracy, paraphrase robustness, adversarial robustness, and calibration. We release Principle-Bench, 168 cryptoasset financial-promotion scenarios mapped to two UK FCA principles, with paraphrase, adversarial keyword-stuffing, and boundary perturbations authored under a pre-registered rubric; the first benchmark covering all four axes for principle-based regulation. We also introduce Ceca (Calibrated Exemplar-Cluster Assessment): a calibrated, auditable assessor that emits exact per-exemplar counterfactual attributions. Across keyword counting, three sentence-transformer embedders, an open-weight LLM-judge, and a calibrated cascade, no method dominates all four axes. A 120B LLM-judge, strongest on benign inputs, loses 47 accuracy points (0.74 to 0.27) on keyword-stuffed Consumer Duty inputs: "compliance theatre." A second judge from a different model family agrees only at Cohen's kappa = 0.16 on that split, localising the failure to the model rather than the corpus. Any deployment-grade LLM-judge for principle-based regulation must report per-principle adversarial deception and post-hoc calibration alongside aggregate accuracy.

</details>


### [118] [A Hybrid LLM-Based Framework for Automated Security Annotation Generation in Business Process Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14370)
*Md Kamrul Islam,Tiphaine Henry,Mattia Salnitri,Julius Köpke,Sami Souihi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: The modelling and analysis of secure business processes require the incorporation of security annotations into process models. Although BPMN extensions, including SecBPMN2, exist for this purpose, the derivation of accurate and complete security annotations from natural-language specifications remains a manual, expert-intensive, and error-prone task. This paper presents a hybrid framework that takes a BPMN process model and a security requirements document as input and automatically generates security annotations adhering to the SecBPMN2 specification. The approach combines Large Language Model (LLM)--based semantic extraction with schema-constrained mapping, rule-based normalization, and deterministic validation. The framework is evaluated comprehensively on a curated dataset of 27 process models from various domains. The results indicate that it consistently produces structurally valid SecBPMN2 annotations with high schema completeness. Compared to human security analysts, the system achieves substantially higher precision (0.58 vs. 0.29) while maintaining comparable recall (0.52 vs. 0.50) and reduces erroneous or misplaced annotations by nearly 50%. In addition, annotation generation is significantly faster than manual annotation. These findings demonstrate that hybrid LLM- and rule-based automation can reduce modeling effort while improving consistency and reliability, thereby providing a scalable foundation for security-by-design BPM.

</details>


### [119] [STINER: Automated Extraction of Strategic Cyber Threat Intelligence from X](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14418)
*Yasir Ech-Chammakhy,Oussama Azrara,Jaafar Chbili,Anas Motii*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出STINER，一个用于从社交媒体流中提取战略网络威胁情报的专家标注语料库和细粒度实体类型体系，包含2,100条真实告警和8种实体类型。在12种配置下评测9个模型，领域自适应编码器（DarkBERT）以89.33%的严格F1-score最优，超越通用基线和微调大语言模型，并用于欧洲H1 2025威胁态势分析，成功捕捉SafePay勒索软件活动的早期信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: X（前Twitter）已成为战略网络威胁情报（STI）的最快来源，常在正式供应商报告发布数天前承载实时数据泄露公告。但社交媒体非正式且高度不规则的方言使传统命名实体识别（NER）模型难以解析，造成自动化防御系统的盲区，亟需专门针对该领域的实体识别方案。

Method: （1）构建STINER，一个专家标注的2,100条真实告警数据集，定义8种以战略要点为中心的实体类型（如Threat Actor、Sector、Location）；（2）在12种配置下基准测试9个模型，涵盖通用与领域自适应编码器、开放模式抽取和生成式大语言模型（零样本与微调）；（3）基于最优STINER-DarkBERT模型进行欧洲H1 2025威胁态势分析。

Result: 领域自适应编码器DarkBERT达到89.33%的严格F1-score，超越通用基线和微调大语言模型；大语言模型还产生显著更高的推理延迟。欧洲H1 2025分析结果与官方报告对主要目标的报道一致，同时揭示了西班牙攻击的独特可见性特征，并能在SafePay勒索软件活动被供应商回顾性分类之前捕捉其早期信号。

Conclusion: STINER证明了领域自适应编码器在处理社交媒体战略CTI抽取方面的高效性和准确性，优于生成式大语言模型；社交媒体驱动的抽取能提供与传统供应商报告互补的早期信号，为自动化防御系统填补了盲区。

Abstract: Strategic Cyber Threat Intelligence (CTI) focuses on high-level insights, such as identifying targeted industries, attributing attacks to specific ransomware groups, and assessing the scale of data loss. Today, X (formerly Twitter) has become the fastest source for this intelligence, often hosting real-time breach announcements days before formal vendor reports. Converting this raw chatter into actionable intelligence requires navigating a complex linguistic landscape. Conventional Named Entity Recognition (NER) models struggle to parse the informal and highly irregular dialect of social media, creating a blind spot for automated defense systems. To address this challenge, we introduce STINER, a taxonomy and expert-annotated corpus for extracting strategic intelligence from social media streams. We construct a high-quality, expert-annotated dataset of 2,100 real-world alerts and propose a granular taxonomy of eight entity types centered on strategic pivots such as Threat Actor, Sector, and Location. We benchmark nine models across 12 evaluated configurations, spanning general-purpose and domain-adapted encoders, open-schema extraction, and generative LLMs in both zero-shot and fine-tuned settings. Domain-adapted encoders such as DarkBERT reach a strict F1-score of 89.33%, outperforming both general-purpose baselines and fine-tuned Large Language Models, which additionally incur substantially higher inference latency. Leveraging STINER-DarkBERT, we conduct a European threat landscape analysis for H1 2025. Our results align with official reporting on major targets while highlighting the distinct visibility profile of attacks in Spain, and illustrate how social-media-driven extraction can surface early signals of the SafePay ransomware campaign prior to its retrospective characterization in vendor threat landscape reports.

</details>


### [120] [Lower Bounds on Black-Box Constructions of Pseudorandom Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14501)
*Bar Alon,Itai Dinur,Muthuramakrishnan Venkitasubramaniam*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文证明在完全黑盒构造（construction和reduction均为黑盒，且reduction与adversary的交互次数独立于adversary的oracle调用次数）下，从PRG构造PRF需要至少ω(n/log n)次（对输入长度n而言）和ω(in/log in)次（对PRF输入长度in而言）非自适应调用PRG。这一不可能性结果甚至适用于输出仅1比特的弱PRF。此外，对于输出足够长的弱PRF，即使允许自适应查询PRG，也证明了相应的下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GGM构造自1984年以来一直是黑盒构造PRF的标准方法，但其需要ω(log n)次PRG调用。能否用更少的调用（尤其是仅一次调用）构造PRF是基本开放问题。尽管Beimel等人2024年证明了对树构造族GGM最优，但普遍意义上的下界仍未知。本文旨在回答这一基本挑战。

Method: 作者考虑完全黑盒构造（construction和reduction都是黑盒，且reduction与adversary之间的交互次数与adversary在其内部函数上的oracle调用次数无关）。通过分析此类构造中非自适应调用PRG的最小次数，建立了o(n/log n)和o(in/log in)次调用不可能成立的下界。对于输出足够长的弱PRF，还将结果扩展到允许自适应查询的情况。

Result: 证明了不存在任何完全黑盒PRF构造能以o(n/log n)或o(in/log in)次非自适应PRG调用实现，即使对于一比特输出的弱PRF以及在adversary仅能查询i.i.d.均匀随机查询的限制下也成立。对于输出足够长的弱PRF，即使允许自适应查询PRG，同样证明了下界。

Conclusion: GGM构造（在Levin域扩展下）使用ω(log n)次PRG调用，而本文证明在完全黑盒框架下，非自适应调用次数需要达到ω(n/log n)（对n为PRG输入长度）或ω(in/log in)（对in为PRF输入长度），即GGM的调用次数还有很大优化空间，但单次PRG调用的可能性被排除。

Abstract: In their seminal work, Goldreich, Goldwasser, and Micali [CRYPTO 1984] constructed a pseudorandom function (PRF) using a black-box access to a pseudorandom generator (PRG). When combined with Levin's domain extension technique, the GGM construction invokes the PRG $ω(\log n)$ times, where $n$ denotes the input length to the PRG. To this day, no black-box construction achieving fewer calls is known.
  Recently, Beimel, Malkin, and Mazor [CRYPTO 2024] showed that for a certain family of constructions, which they termed \emph{tree constructions}, the GGM construction is optimal. However, the basic challenge of whether a PRF can be built with just \emph{one invocation} of the PRG still remains open.
  In this work, we consider fully black-box constructions of PRFs from PRGs, where both the construction and the reduction are required to be black-box, and the number of interactions the reduction makes with the adversary is independent of the number of oracle calls the adversary makes to its underlying function within each interaction. Our main result shows that no such construction can have $o(n/\log n)$ and $o(\mathsf{in}/\log\mathsf{in})$ \emph{non-adaptive} calls to the PRG, where $\mathsf{in}$ is the input length of the PRF. This impossibility holds even for weak PRFs with one-bit output, where the adversary is restricted to making i.i.d. uniformly random queries. In addition, we prove a lower bound for weak PRFs with sufficiently long outputs that holds even when the construction is allowed to make adaptive queries to the PRG.

</details>


### [121] [Trust Without Boundaries: An Architectural Analysis of Satellite Flight Software](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14532)
*Jack Vanlyssel,Gruia-Catalin Roman,Kendra Cook,Sazzadur Rahaman,Afsah Anwar*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文分析了NASA的核心飞行软件（cFS）架构中的信任假设与安全弱点，通过NOS3模拟器中的五个实验证明，单一被攻破的组件可利用其合法架构特权（如共享权限、身份不隔离）实施难以与正常行为区分的恶意操作，并与其他模块化飞行软件框架对比，揭示了普遍存在的信任缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着航天器日益软件化和互联化，机载飞行软件成为重要的安全边界。然而主流飞行软件架构常将机载组件视为互信的对等实体，简化了集成过程却限制了内部隔离与访问控制，造成潜在安全风险。

Method: 对NASA核心飞行软件（cFS）进行架构分析，从权威（authority）、身份（identity）、通信（communication）、可观测性（observability）和持久性（persistence）五个维度剖析组件间权限分布；使用NASA飞行代表性的NOS3模拟器，通过一个恶意机载组件滥用合法架构特权实施五个安全实验来验证弱点；最后与其他模块化飞行软件框架进行对比，识别重复出现的信任假设与架构弱点。

Result: 实验表明，单个被攻陷的组件可以利用广泛共享的权威执行恶意行为，且这些行为与合法操作难以区分；对比分析发现多个模块化飞行软件框架存在相似的信任假设和架构缺陷，属于系统性问题。

Conclusion: 文章提出了架构层面的改进建议，并讨论了在未来飞行软件系统中强化内部信任边界的机制，以提升航天器飞行软件的整体安全性。

Abstract: As spacecraft become more software-driven and interconnected, onboard flight software is an increasingly important security boundary. Popular flight software architectures often treat onboard components as trusted peers, simplifying integration while limiting internal isolation and access control. We analyze NASA's Core Flight Software (cFS) to examine how authority, identity, communication, observability, and persistence are distributed across onboard components. Using NASA's flight-representative NOS3 simulator, we validate these weaknesses through five experiments implemented with a malicious onboard component that abuses legitimate architectural privileges. We then compare cFS with other modular flight software frameworks to identify recurring trust assumptions and architectural weaknesses. Our results show that a single compromised component can exploit broadly shared authority in ways that are difficult to distinguish from legitimate behavior. We conclude with architectural implications and discuss mechanisms for strengthening internal trust boundaries in future flight software systems.

</details>


### [122] [Finding Vulnerabilities via LLM-Augmented Semantics-Aware Type-Checking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14533)
*Ruizhe Wang,Meng Xu,N. Asokan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出SETYPE，一种基于语义感知的类型系统，通过LLM直接从源代码中推断和检查类型，将失败的类型检查作为漏洞指示器。原型PYSETYPE在Python Web应用中达到87%的检测精度和88%的检测准确率，并发现了15个潜在零日漏洞，其中9个被开发者确认。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于静态分析的漏洞检测依赖安全专家手工编码不安全模式为规则，但这些规则通常只关注语法模式，忽略了代码中的深层语义信息（如变量名和函数名的含义）。随着软件系统日益复杂，仅使用语法规则建模漏洞变得越来越困难。

Method: 提出SETYPE语义感知类型系统，直接从源代码中基于自然语言中符号和表达式的含义推导得到。类型推断和类型检查均由大语言模型（LLM）执行，类型检查失败即表示存在潜在漏洞。并实现了原型PYSETYPE用于检测Python Web应用中的漏洞。

Result: 在真实世界应用上的评估达到了87%的检测精度和88%的检测准确率。使用PYSETYPE识别出15个潜在零日漏洞，其中9个已获开发者确认。

Conclusion: 语义感知的LLM驱动类型系统可以有效弥补传统语法规则方法的不足，在真实应用中表现出良好的检测能力，并具备发现零日漏洞的实用价值。

Abstract: Vulnerability detection via static analysis traditionally relies on security experts encoding insecure coding patterns into algorithmic rules.
